# Agents · R · part 9

965 entries, researcher to retrospective-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **researcher**: Worker. Deep research on one specific, bounded question. Sources every claim with URL + date + confidence. Returns structured findings JSON to Research-Lead. Never invents data. ([Adam077K/Beamix](https://github.com/Adam077K/Beamix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Worker. Deep research on one specific question. Sources every claim. Returns structured findings with confidence levels to Research Lead. Called by Research Lead. - **researcher**: Read-only exploration that reports facts with citations. Use to locate the code a change touches, to find how something currently works, to check prior changes, ADRs and incidents for a subject, or to gather external facts from documentation. Returns findings with file and line, commit, or URL for each, and lists what it could not find. Never edits, never recommends. ([AdamAkhlaq/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/AdamAkhlaq/claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Resolves the brief's unknowns with sourced evidence. Every claim carries a source_url fetched on this run. ([Adi-gitX/power](https://github.com/Adi-gitX/power/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Competitive analysis and market research agent. - **researcher**: Explores a codebase to answer specific questions or build context for a new feature. Returns a structured summary. Use this when context-building would otherwise pollute the main session. ([Chawengwit/PPY](https://github.com/Chawengwit/PPY/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Invoke when company or JD research is needed. Gathers company information, JD requirements, tech stack, etc. via web search. ([chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins](https://github.com/chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/resume/.claude/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Independent research subagent for SPADES. - **researcher**: 웹을 탐색해 출처를 모으고 검증을 통과한 행으로 근거 지도를 만드는 연구자다. 호출 절차가 이름으로 지정할 때만 실행한다. ([chshin84/disciplined-coder](https://github.com/chshin84/disciplined-coder/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Use when a ck-code skill needs external facts before deciding — a library's current version, idiomatic API usage, a migration note, a known bug or an approach comparison — gathered from context7, package registries and the web on a cheap model and returned as sourced findings, so the main agent never researches itself. ([ckandrinirina/ck-code](https://github.com/ckandrinirina/ck-code/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use when a ck-code-lite skill needs external facts before deciding — a library's current version, idiomatic API usage, a migration note, a known bug or an approach comparison — gathered from context7, package registries and the web on a cheap model and returned as sourced findings, so the main agent never researches itself. ([ckandrinirina/ck-code-lite](https://github.com/ckandrinirina/ck-code-lite/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use this agent to investigate genuine unknowns — anything the team can't answer from what it already knows: a library's current API or version behavior, an external standard, a "how does X actually work" question that spans the web and the repo. It returns cited findings and their implications for the spec or plan, never a guess. The conductor dispatches it when a task hinges on a fact nobody on the team can confirm from memory or stale training. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: A plan depends on how a third-party library behaves in its current version. user: "We're on Stripe's API — does their n… ([Codestz/agentry](https://github.com/Codestz/agentry/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Knowledge-gap filler for any phase. Does not modify the repository — it gathers only. Note it is not sandboxed: it holds WebFetch/WebSearch egress and runs the Context7 CLI via npx, so it fetches remote content and executes an npm package. Returns a synthesized answer with sources. ([Concord-Voice/agentic-dev-tooling](https://github.com/Concord-Voice/agentic-dev-tooling/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-lifecycle/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: 調査担当。実装前の仕様調査・実装後のworktreeブランチ差分の事実確認を行う。 実装agentが何を書いたか事前に知らない状態でdiffだけを読む。 Use when: reading mockup HTML files to extract components before implementation, verifying facts in worktree branch diff after implementation (what was actually changed vs spec). ([Cooker-fisher/kanto-fishing](https://github.com/Cooker-fisher/kanto-fishing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Phase 1에서 문서 하나당 하나씩 띄운다. 펜스 안이 그대로 넘어가는 프롬프트 본문이고, 대괄호 슬롯만 채운다. 아래 세 줄은 넘기지 않는다. ([Cotidie/cotidie-skills](https://github.com/Cotidie/cotidie-skills/blob/HEAD/claude/skills/explain-concept/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)

- **researcher**: Read-only investigation across a codebase and/or the web. Returns a synthesized answer, never edits. ([CSparks/claude-kit](https://github.com/CSparks/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research agent — comprehensive package, license, pattern, and reference project research. - **researcher**: Owns the `research` phase — the first phase in every pipeline. Produces a dated findings doc that the brainstormer (project) or architect (feature) consumes as upstream input. Does NOT make product or engineering decisions — it surfaces evidence and open questions. Outputs a dated markdown spec under the scope's research directory. - **researcher**: Deep multi source research agent. Spawn it to investigate a question thoroughly, covering technical options, market and competitor landscape, API capabilities, best practices, or "how do other people solve X", across web sources and local files. Returns a synthesized answer with cited sources, explicit confidence levels, and clear separation of fact from inference. Use for any research task meaty enough to justify a cold start, or when several research questions can run in parallel. ([ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit](https://github.com/ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research agent. - **Researcher**: Generic research agent. Use this when no methodology-specific researcher (academic / investigative / contrarian / multi-angle / deep-investigation) is a clearly better fit. ([devergehq/locus](https://github.com/devergehq/locus/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))

([eviefang/career-plan](https://github.com/eviefang/career-plan/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Investigates a scoped question in isolation and returns a tight summary, not a raw dump. Use for unfamiliar code, "how does X work", or gathering context before planning. ([K95M65/AI_ONBOARD](https://github.com/K95M65/AI_ONBOARD/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers and evaluates sources on a focused question and returns condensed, CITED findings — a read-only fan-out gatherer dispatched in parallel (×N). Returns summarized findings with source URLs and dates; never writes the final report. Do NOT use to synthesize or write the final research report, integrate multiple researchers' findings, or run the citation gate — use research-synthesizer instead. - **rethink-gutachter**: Gutachter und Tor für Spezifikationsdateien im Rethink-Prozess. Liest eine neue oder geänderte Bereichsdatei beziehungsweise einen Testauftrag und beurteilt, ob sie den Maßstab erfüllt — Beleg je Regel, Absicherung oder Lücke, zwei Quellen, Abwesenheitsaussagen mit ausgeführter Maske, Zahlen mit Zähleinheit, Zuschnitt ohne Doppelbesitz, Abnahmekriterium wörtlich. Ist ein Tor vor der Veröffentlichung — ohne seine Freigabe wird nichts veröffentlicht. Meldet Funde, ändert nichts. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-gutachter.md))
- **rethink-umsetzer**: Umsetzer im Rethink-Prozess. Misst am Bestand, schreibt Bereichsdateien und Testaufträge, baut absichernde Tests mit Negativkontrolle und Mutationsprobe, behebt Torfunde. Arbeitet je Block gegen einen benannten Messstand, belegt jede Aussage mit Datei und Zeile oder mit einer ausgeführten Maske, und veröffentlicht erst nach dem Ja des Tores. Meldet Vollzug nach jedem Block, wartet sofort bei jeder Entscheidung. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-umsetzer.md))
- **rethink-zahlenpruefer**: Zahlenprüfer im Rethink-Prozess. Läuft vor jeder Torrunde über die im Block geänderten Dateien und prüft jede Zahl, jede Aufzählung und jede Zeilenangabe gegen ihre Maske — die ausgeführt und nicht gelesen wird — sowie gegen dieselbe Angabe in allen übrigen Dateien. Greppt zusätzlich nach Ausschließlichkeitsaussagen. Liefert eine Tabelle, kein Urteil; der Block geht erst ins Tor, wenn sie leer ist. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-zahlenpruefer.md))
